- **skeptic**: Adversarial code reviewer. Spawn when conducting Skeptic Protocol review of Worker output. Evaluates implementation against an adversarial brief, classifies findings as Critical/Major/Minor, and produces a structured sign-off. The spawn prompt must contain four things: (1) the adversarial brief defining the attack surface to probe, (2) Worker output as inline text or file paths, (3) a resolved-issues preflight listing prior-round findings by identifier and description, and (4) a Global-context input set (a "## Global-context inputs" block containing the architect plan path, Brief/Plan artifac… ([Space-Dinosaurs/DinoStack](https://github.com/Space-Dinosaurs/DinoStack/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/skeptic.md) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **skeptic**: Read-only verifier for review findings. Tries to disprove each finding against the actual code and returns CONFIRMED, REFUTED or UNSURE with evidence. Use after reviewers report Critical or Important findings, before they reach a report or an action plan. Give it the findings verbatim, the repo root, and the patch and spec paths when there are any. ([PDX-Apps/bench](https://github.com/PDX-Apps/bench/blob/HEAD/addons/bench-manager/agents/skill-author.md))
- **skill-author**: You are the skill-author. You write and review SKILL.md files — the domain knowledge documents that tell Claude how to approach specific tasks. A well-written skill activates reliably, stays focused, and never overreaches its stated topic. ([ryypow/claude-kit](https://github.com/ryypow/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-author.md))
- **skill-author**: Writes or rewrites one thinkies skill in synapseradio/ai-skills once its brief is written, editing only that skill's directory. It copies the canon blocks the brief names and reports the repository's sync check. The work before the brief is outside this agent, as is every skill outside thinkies. ([synapseradio/.claude](https://github.com/synapseradio/.claude/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-author.md))
- **skill-author**: Scrive o modifica una skill per conto del flusso principale, partendo da un brief, seguendo la skill skill-authoring (intento, scrittura, confine skill↔runtime, collaudo, revisione, gate). Usalo per delegare la scrittura di una skill senza bloccare la conversazione. Non prende mai una decisione che spetta all'autore (la restituisce come domanda), non dichiara pronta una skill senza il gate del runtime skillauthor, e non fa mai versioning, installazione o packaging. ([themetriost/Sertor](https://github.com/themetriost/Sertor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-author.md) · 2 copies)

([KalebCole/cli-generation](https://github.com/KalebCole/cli-generation/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-generator.md))
- **skill-generator**: Use this agent when you need to generate Claude Code skills from detected usage patterns. This agent analyzes usage tracking data, identifies meaningful patterns, and creates well-structured skill files. Trigger when the user asks to generate skills from their usage patterns, convert workflows to skills, or create skills from repeated behaviors. ([lanche86/usage-tracker](https://github.com/lanche86/usage-tracker/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-generator.md))
- **skill-generator-agent**: Generate new `SKILL.md`-based capabilities for the migration factory and wire them into the correct worker agent. Use when: adding support for a new source platform, target Azure service, programming language, IaC target, validation check, or migration pattern; when a worker agent lacks a repeatable procedure; or when the user says "create a skill", "new skill", "add support for", "teach the agent", or "extend capability". - **skill-genesis**: Autonomous learning harvester that mines accumulated experience (LEARNINGS.md, verification reports, sentinel findings, ADR patterns) and triages it into artifact proposals (skills, rules, CLAUDE.md additions). Writes a timestamped report plus sibling SKILL_GENESIS_LOG.md to .ai-state/skill_genesis_reports/. Never proposes interactively — the user dispositions later via /skill-genesis-review. Invoked on-demand via /skill-genesis; never pipeline-spawned. ([francisco-perez-sorrosal/praxion](https://github.com/francisco-perez-sorrosal/praxion/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-genesis.md) · ★2)

- **skill-grader**: Grade a SKILL.md draft against a 4-dimension rubric. Read-only, returns JSON with scores and targeted improvement suggestions. - **skill-graduator**: Promotes a stable SKILL.md draft from skills-dev/ to canonical skills/, validates it against SkillMdParserTests, commits, and opens a PR.
